## EventID
```sql
powershell_operational D
SELECT Event.System.Computer, Event.System.EventID, COUNT(*)
FROM read_json('C:/Users/SANSDFIR/powershell_operational.json', map_inference_threshold = -1)
WHERE Event.System.TimeCreated."#attributes".SystemTime >= TIMESTAMP '2023-01-01 00:00:00' AND Event.System.TimeCreated."#attributes".SystemTime <  TIMESTAMP '2023-02-01 00:00:00'
GROUP BY ALL
ORDER BY ALL;

## 4103
```sql

```
fullkey
```sql
powershell_operational D
SELECT je.fullkey, COUNT(*)
FROM read_json_objects('C:/Users/SANSDFIR/powershell_operational.json') AS e, json_tree(e.json) AS je
WHERE e.json->>'$.Event.System.EventID' = '4103' AND je.fullkey LIKE '$.Event.EventData.%'
GROUP BY je.fullkey
ORDER BY je.fullkey;

FROM read_json('C:/Users/SANSDFIR/powershell_operational.json', map_inference_threshold = -1)

│ rd01.shieldbase.com │ C:\Program Files\Amazon\Ec2ConfigService\Scripts\DiscoverConsolePort.ps1            │             1 │            1 │ # Copyright 2017 Amazon.com, Inc. or its affiliates. All Rights Reserved.\n# version 1.0.0.1\n\n# For platforms where in the PCIe UART (described in ACPI SPCR)\n# gets mapped to a different symbolic link COMx (where x != 1),\n# the below function discovers the corresponding COMx and returns it.\n\n# Read ACPI SPCR table to find the Device Information for the PCIe UART\n# AWSReadAcpiSpcr.exe is a Win32 console which uses the ACPI Table info\n# to extract the right COM Port to use.\n\n#\n# Function to return the B/D/F for the passed LocationInformation \n# from RegistryHive\n#\nfunction FindBDF\n{\n    param ([string]$LocationInfo)\n    [hashtable]$result = @{ }\n    \n    [void]($LocationInfo -match "\d{1,},\d{1,},\d{1,}")\n    $busId, $deviceID, $functionID = $matches[0] -split ","\n    $result.busId = $busId\n    $result.deviceID = $deviceID\n    $result.functionID = $functionID\n    return $result\n    \n}\n\n#\n# This function takes all of the information from ACPI SPCR\n# and returns the matching MF device.\n#\nfunction FindMatchingPCISerialDevice\n{\n    param ([string]$VendId,\n        [string]$DeviceId,\n        [string]$PCIBusId,\n        [string]$PCIDeviceId,\n        [string]$PCIFunctionId)\n    \n    try\n    {\n        #PCI info needs to be converted to base 10 for downstream compare\n        $PCIBusId = [Convert]::ToInt64($PCIBusId,16)\n        $PCIDeviceId = [Convert]::ToInt64($PCIDeviceId,16)\n        $PCIFunctionId = [Convert]::ToInt64($PCIFunctionId,16)\n\n        $DeviceIdSubstring = "VEN_" + $VendId + "&" + "DEV_" + $DeviceId\n        [string]$MFDeviceIdSubstring = ''\n        $MatchingMFDevice = $null\n        $locationInfo = $null\n        Get-WmiObject -Query 'Select * from Win32_PnPEntity' | where  { $_.DeviceID -Match $DeviceIdSubstring } |\n        ForEach-Object -Process {\n            if ($_.DeviceID.StartsWith("PCI"))\n            {\n                $DevId = $_.DeviceID\n                $locationInfo = (get-itemproperty -path "HKLM:\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Enum\$DevId" `\n                                                  -name LocationInformation).Locat │            4 │
│                     │                                                                                     │               │              │ ionInformation\n                if ($locationInfo -eq $null)\n                {\n                    continue\n                }\n                # Retrieve BDF values\t\t\t\t\n                $DeviceBDF = FindBDF $locationInfo\n                if (($DeviceBDF.busId -eq $PCIBusId) -And ($DeviceBDF.deviceID -eq $PCIDeviceId) -and ($DeviceBDF.functionId -eq $PCIFunctionId))\n                {\n                    # Matching device found which matches all the parameters from SPCR\n                    # Retrieve the substring after the prefix PCI/ and exclude the child portion\n                    # This is then used to search under the MF device tree\t\t\t\t\t\n                    $MFDeviceIdSubstring = $DevId.split("\")[1]\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if ($MFDeviceIdSubstring -eq '')\n        {\n            return $MatchingMFDevice\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            Get-WmiObject -Query 'Select * from Win32_PnPEntity' | where  { $_.DeviceID -Match $MFDeviceIdSubstring } |\n            ForEach-Object -Process {\n                # Device must be belong to the MF tree\t\t\t\t\n                if ($_.DeviceID.StartsWith("MF"))\n                {\n                    $MatchingMFDevice = $_\n                }\n                \n            }\n        }\n    }\n    catch\n    {\n        Write-Verbose "Unexpected Error during MF device lookup."\n        return $MatchingMFDevice\n    }\n    return $MatchingMFDevice\n}\n\n# This function reads the SPCR table and then uses that \n# information to return the mapped COM port.\n#\n\nfunction DiscoverConsolePort()\n{\n    [cmdletbinding()]\n    param()\n\n    # Default COM port is COM1\n    $DefaultTargetPortName = "COM1"\n    \n    if ([Environment]::OSVersion.Version.Major -lt 6)\n    {\n        # Do not run this code on Major versions less than 6\n        return $DefaultTargetPortName\n    }\n    \n    try\n    {\n        $SPCRReaderExeDir = $PSScriptRoot\n        if (Test-Path -Path $SPCRReaderExeDir)\n        {\n            $SPCRReaderExePath = Join-Path $SPCRReaderExeDir "AWSAcpiSpcrReader.exe" │              │
│                     │                                                                                     │               │              │ \n            $SPCROutput = cmd /c $SPCRReaderExePath\n            \n            # Parse the SPCR output and extract key fields such as Vendor ID, Device ID, BDF.\n            $Dictionary = @{ }\n            if ($SPCROutput -match 'SPCR table signature')\n            {\n                foreach ($line in $SPCROutput)\n                {\n                    $key, $value = $line.Trim().Split(':')\n                    $Dictionary[$key.Trim()] = $value.Trim()\n                }\n                \n                if (!($Dictionary.ContainsKey("VendorId") -and $Dictionary.ContainsKey("DeviceID")))\n                {\t\n                    $Message = "Missing Vendor ID or device ID"\n                    Write-Verbose $Message\n                    return $DefaultTargetPortName\n                }\n                \n                if (!($Dictionary.ContainsKey("Bus") -and $Dictionary.ContainsKey("Device") -and $Dictionary.ContainsKey("Function")))\n                {\t\n                    $Message = "Missing Bus or Device of Function"\n                    Write-Verbose $Message\n                    return $DefaultTargetPortName\n                }\n                \n                # Check if a matching MF device does exit\n                $Device = FindMatchingPCISerialDevice $Dictionary["VendorID"] $Dictionary["DeviceID"] $Dictionary["Bus"] $Dictionary["Device"] $Dictionary["Function"]\n                \n                if ($Device -eq $null)\n                {\n                    $Message = "No Matching UART device found"\n                    Write-Verbose $Message\n                    exit 1\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    $PortKey = "HKLM:\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Enum\" + $Device.DeviceID + "\Device Parameters"\n                    $Port = get-itemproperty -path $PortKey -Name PortName -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n                    if (($Port -eq $null) -Or ($Port.Length -eq 0) -Or ($Port.PortName.Length -eq 0))\n                    {\t\n                        $Message = "COM PortName is not valid"\n                        Write-Ver │              │
│                     │                                                                                     │               │              │ bose $Message\n                        exit 1\n                    }\n                    else\n                    {\n                        # Found a matching COM port in the ENUM registry hive.\n                        Write-Verbose "Matching UART device found"\n                        $TargetPortName = $Port.PortName\n                        return $TargetPortName\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            else\n            {\t\n                Write-Verbose  "ACPI SPCR table not found. Bailing Out"\n                return $DefaultTargetPortName\n            }\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            Write-Verbose 'SPCR reader Exe is not present. Exiting'\n            return $DefaultTargetPortName\n        }\n    }\n    catch\n    {\n        Write-Verbose "Unexpected Error. Return the default Port COM1"\n        return $DefaultTargetPortName\n    }\n    return $DefaultTargetPortName\n}\n\nDiscoverConsolePort                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      │              │

See the License for the specific language governing\n# permissions and limitations under the License.\n\n<#-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    Send-FeatureStatus sends feature installation and corresponding service status to the console.\n-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------#>\n\nfunction Send-FeatureStatus\n{\n    set-variable -Name INSTALL_STATE_ENABLED -Value 1 -Option Constant\n    try\n    {\n        $windowsFeatures = @( @{ FeatureName = "Microsoft-Hyper-V"; InstalledServiceName = "vmms" } )\n        \n        $featureName = ""\n        \n        foreach($feature in $windowsFeatures)\n        {\n            $featureName = $feature.FeatureName\n            $featureInfo =  Get-WmiObject -query "select * from Win32_OptionalFeature where name = '$featureName'" -ErrorAction "SilentlyContinue"\n            if($featureInfo.InstallState -eq $INSTALL_STATE_ENABLED)\n            {\n                Write-Output ("Message: {0} feature is installed." -f $feature.FeatureName)\n                \n                $srvStatus = (Get-Service -Name $feature.InstalledServiceName -ErrorAction "SilentlyContinue").Status\n                if($srvStatus)\n                {\n                    Write-Output ("Message: {0} service ({1}) is {2}." -f $feature.FeatureName, $feature.InstalledServiceName, $srvStatus)\n                }\n                else\n                {\n                    Write-Output ("Message: Unable to get service status for {0}." -f $feature.InstalledServiceName )\n                } │            4 │
│                     │                                                                                     │               │              │ \n\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    catch\n    {\n        Write-Output ("Message: Failed to get info for feature {0}." -f $featureName) \n    }\n}\n\nSend-FeatureStatus\n   Delete-OldFolders (Get-Item ($folder.FullName))\r\n            }\r\n        }\r\n    }\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Function to get registry value\r\nfunction Get-RegistryValue([string]$registryEntryPath = $(throw return $result.count\r\n    }\r\n    else\r\n    {\r\n        return 0\r\n    }\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Function to convert to WQL path\r\nfunction ConvertTo-WQLPath([string]$wqlPath = $(throw "No WQL path is QueryEntry(ref Guid pguidGrp, string pszPath, ref UAINFO pui);\r\n        int SetEntry(ref Guid pguidGrp, string pszPath, │              │

Guid("DD313E04-FEFF-11d1-8ECD-0000F87A470C")]\r\n    internal class UserAssist { }\r\n\r\n    public static DateTime GetLastAccessTime(string filePath)\r\n    {\r\n        if(String.IsNullOrEmpty(filePath))\r\n        {\r\n            throw new ArgumentException("The file path is null or empty");\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        UAINFO uaInfo = new UAINFO(UAIM_FILETIME);\r\n        IShellUserAssist iShellUserAssist = new UserAssist() as IShellUserAssist;\r\n        if (iShellUserAssist == null)\r\n        {\r\n            throw new InvalidOperationException("Can't get iShellUserAssist interface");\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        try\r\n        {\r\n            Marshal.ThrowExceptionForHR(iShellUserAssist.QueryEntry(ref UAIID_SHORTCUTS, filePath, ref uaInfo));\r\n        }\r\n        catch\r\n        {\r\n            throw new InvalidOperationException("Can't query info about" + filePath);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        long fileTime = (((long)uaInfo.ftExecute.dwHighDateTime) << 32) + uaInfo.ftExecute.dwLowDateTime;\r\n\r\n        return DateTime.FromFileTime(fileTime);\r\n    }\r\n}\r\n"@\r\n\r\n    $type = Add-Type -TypeDefinition $typeDefinition -PassThru\r\n\r\n    return $type[0]::GetLastAccessTime($filePath)\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Function to check whether the icon is pointing to a file\r\nfunction Test-FileShortcut([Wmi]$wmiLinkFile = $(throw "No wmi link file is specified"))\r\n{\r\n    if($wmiLinkFile -eq $null)\r\n    {\r\n        return $false\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    [string]$target = choice value is specified"), [xml]$extension)\r\n{\r\n    return @{"Name"=$name;"Description"=$description;"Value"=$value;"ExtensionPoint"=$extension.InnerXml}\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Function to check whether the current machine is domain joined\r\nFunction Test-DomainJoined()\r\n{\r\n    return (Get-WmiObject -query "select * from win32_ntdomain where Status ='OK'") -ne $null\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Function to Function to get system drive info\r\nfunction Get-SystemDriveInfo() {\r\n    [string]$wql = "SELECT * FROM Win32_LogicalDisk WHERE MediaType=12 AND Name = '" + ${env:systemdrive} + "'"\r\n    return Get-WmiObject -query $wql\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Function to get time service status\r\nfunction Get-ServiceStatus([string]$serviceName=$(throw "No service name is specified")) {\r\n   [bool]$startService = $true\r\n\r\n   [WMI]$timeService = @(Get-WmiObject -Query "Select * From Win32_Service Where Name = `"$serviceName`"")[0]\r\n   if($null -ne $timeService) {\r\n      [ServiceProcess.ServiceControllerStatus]$timeServicesStatus = (Get-Service $serviceName).Status\r\n      if(([ServiceProcess.ServiceControllerStatus]::Stopped -eq $timeServicesStatus) -or ([ServiceProcess.ServiceControllerStatus]::StopPending -eq $timeServicesStatus)) {\r\n         $startService = $false\r\n      }\r\n   }\r\n\r\n   return $startService\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Function to wait for expected service status\r\nfunction WaitFor-ServiceStatus([string]$serviceName=$(throw "No service name is specified"), [ServiceProcess.ServiceControllerStatus]$serviceStatus=$(throw "No service status is specified")) {\r\n    [ServiceProcess.ServiceController]$sc = New-Object "ServiceProcess.ServiceController" $serviceName\r\n    [TimeSpan]$timeOut = New-Object TimeSpan(0,0,0,5,0)\r\n    $sc.WaitForStatus($serviceStatus, $timeOut)\r\n}                                                                            │              │

fullkey
```sql
powershell_operational D
SELECT je.fullkey, COUNT(*)
FROM read_json_objects('C:/Users/SANSDFIR/powershell_operational.json') AS e, json_tree(e.json) AS je
WHERE e.json->>'$.Event.System.EventID' = '4104' AND je.fullkey LIKE '$.Event.EventData.%'
GROUP BY je.fullkey
ORDER BY je.fullkey;
┌───────────────────────────────────┬──────────────┐
│              fullkey              │ count_star() │
│              varchar              │    int64     │
├───────────────────────────────────┼──────────────┤
│ $.Event.EventData.MessageNumber   │         4502 │
│ $.Event.EventData.MessageTotal    │         4502 │
│ $.Event.EventData.Path            │         4502 │
│ $.Event.EventData.ScriptBlockId   │         4502 │
│ $.Event.EventData.ScriptBlockText │         4502 │
└───────────────────────────────────┴──────────────┘
